--- [ATTEMPT] prompt @ 15:29:29.856 ---
"You are a **smoking quit-attempt extractor** analyzing a tobacco-cessation counseling conversation.\n\nCONVERSATION:\nPatient: Hi\nCounselor: Hello! I'm eDOSTHI, your friendly AI coach. What brings you here today? Are you thinking about quitting smoking or already on that journey?\nPatient: I wish to quit smoking\nCounselor: That's a huge step! Congratulations on taking the first one. What's been the hardest part for you about quitting so far?\n\nYour task is to identify all **prior quit attempts** mentioned by the **patient**.\n\nA **quit attempt** is any instance where the patient **intentionally tried to stop or reduce smoking**, regardless of success or duration.\n\nThis includes:\n\n* Stopping abruptly (e.g., cold turkey)\n* Using aids (nicotine patch, gum, medication, vaping to quit, etc.)\n* Gradually reducing smoking with the intention to quit\n* Temporary abstinence (even for a short period such as hours, days, or weeks)\n* Any structured or unstructured effort (self-initiated or guided)\n\nDo NOT include:\n\n* General thoughts, desires, or intentions to quit without action\n* Future plans to quit\n* Hypothetical or vague statements without an actual attempt\n\nFor each valid quit attempt, extract:\n\n* Method used (if not mentioned, use \"unknown\")\n* Duration (if not mentioned, use \"unknown\")\n* Reason for relapse or stopping (if not mentioned, use \"unknown\")\n\nRepresent each attempt as a **single concise natural-language description**.\n\nAlso extract **evidence**:\n\n* Provide an exact quote or a close paraphrase from the conversation that supports each attempt\n* Evidence must correspond to the same order as the attempts\n\nAdditional rules:\n\n* Focus only on statements made by the patient, not the counselor\n* Do not infer missing details; use \"unknown\" when necessary\n* If multiple attempts are mentioned in a single sentence, separate them into distinct attempts\n* If no attempts are found, return empty lists\n\nOutput strictly in valid JSON with the following schema:\n\n{\n\"attempts\": [\"<attempt description 1>\", \"<attempt description 2>\"],\n\"evidence\": [\"<supporting quote/paraphrase 1>\", \"<supporting quote/paraphrase 2>\"]\n}\n"
